Conclusion: 该框架为在LLM预测可用时优化人类受访者分配提供了系统方法，能显著提高估计效率，且适用于广义M-估计问题，包括回归系数和联合分析中的多项logit部分价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models can generate synthetic survey responses at low cost, but their accuracy varies unpredictably across questions. We study the design problem of allocating a fixed budget of human respondents across estimation tasks when cheap LLM predictions are available for every task. Our framework combines three components. First, building on Prediction-Powered Inference, we characterize a question-specific rectification difficulty that governs how quickly the estimator's variance decreases with human sample size. Second, we derive a closed-form optimal allocation rule that directs more human labels to tasks where the LLM is least reliable. Third, since rectification difficulty depends on unobserved human responses for new surveys, we propose a meta-learning approach, trained on historical data, that predicts it for entirely new tasks without pilot data. The framework extends to general M-estimation, covering regression coefficients and multinomial logit partworths for conjoint analysis. We validate the framework on two datasets spanning different domains, question types, and LLMs, showing that our approach captures 61-79% of the theoretically attainable efficiency gains, achieving 11.4% and 10.5% MSE reductions without requiring any pilot human data for the target survey.

</details>


### [114] [The Continuity Layer: Why Intelligence Needs an Architecture for What It Carries Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17273)
*Samuel Sameer Tanguturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AI领域最重要的架构问题是缺乏"连续性层"——一个能够跨会话和时间传递模型理解内容的持久层，而非模型规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统存在"会话性遗忘"问题：会话结束、上下文窗口填满、内存API返回的平面事实需要模型每次重新解释，导致智能在单个会话中强大但跨时间失忆。作者认为解决这一问题的连续性层是AI领域尚未构建的最重要基础设施。

Method: 提出连续性作为具有七个必要特征的系统属性（区别于记忆和检索）；设计存储原语"分解轨迹收敛内存"，通过写时分解和读时重构实现连续性；建立形式化评估框架ATANT基准；将工程架构映射到神学模式（虚己）和符号模式（阿尔法与欧米伽）；提出四层发展路径：外部SDK→硬件节点→长期人类基础设施；分析物理限制如何使连续性层变得关键；讨论治理架构（隐私作为物理而非政策实现，创始人对架构承诺的非可协商控制权）。

Result: 提出了完整的连续性层理论框架和实现方法，包括ATANT基准（在250个故事语料上评估）、存储原语设计、架构映射和发展路径。工程工作已公开开始，治理架构与产品本身不可分割。

Conclusion: 连续性层是AI领域尚未构建的最重要基础设施，其工程实现已开始。该层不仅解决跨时间记忆问题，其治理架构（隐私作为物理实现、创始人对架构承诺的控制）与产品本身不可分割，代表了AI系统架构的根本性进步。

Abstract: The most important architectural problem in AI is not the size of the model but the absence of a layer that carries forward what the model has come to understand. Sessions end. Context windows fill. Memory APIs return flat facts that the model has to reinterpret from scratch on every read. The result is intelligence that is powerful per session and amnesiac across time. This position paper argues that the layer which fixes this, the continuity layer, is the most consequential piece of infrastructure the field has not yet built, and that the engineering work to build it has begun in public. The formal evaluation framework for the property described here is the ATANT benchmark (arXiv:2604.06710), published separately with evaluation results on a 250-story corpus; a companion paper (arXiv:2604.10981) positions this framework against existing memory, long-context, and agentic-memory benchmarks. The paper defines continuity as a system property with seven required characteristics, distinct from memory and from retrieval; describes a storage primitive (Decomposed Trace Convergence Memory) whose write-time decomposition and read-time reconstruction produce that property; maps the engineering architecture to the theological pattern of kenosis and the symbolic pattern of Alpha and Omega, and argues this mapping is structural rather than metaphorical; proposes a four-layer development arc from external SDK to hardware node to long-horizon human infrastructure; examines why the physics limits now constraining the model layer make the continuity layer newly consequential; and argues that the governance architecture (privacy implemented as physics rather than policy, founder-controlled class shares on non-negotiable architectural commitments) is inseparable from the product itself.

</details>


### [115] [LLaTiSA: Towards Difficulty-Stratified Time Series Reasoning from Visual Perception to Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17295)
*Yueyang Ding,HaoPeng Zhang,Rui Dai,Yi Wang,Tianyu Zong,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个分层时间序列推理框架HiTSR和相应的模型LLaTiSA，通过可视化模式与数值表格结合增强视觉语言模型的时间感知能力，在时间序列推理任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在时间序列理解方面存在挑战，研究受到碎片化任务定义和基准测试模糊性的阻碍，缺乏统一的评估框架和时间序列推理模型。

Method: 1) 通过四层认知复杂度分类法形式化时间序列推理；2) 构建包含83k样本的分层时间序列推理数据集HiTSR；3) 提出LLaTiSA模型，整合可视化模式与精度校准数值表格；4) 采用多阶段课程微调策略。

Result: LLaTiSA在时间序列推理任务上表现出优越性能，并展现出强大的跨分布泛化能力，适用于多样化的TSR任务和实际场景。

Conclusion: 该研究为时间序列推理提供了统一的评估框架和强大的基准模型，通过层次化数据集和视觉-数值融合方法显著提升了模型的时间感知能力。

Abstract: Comprehensive understanding of time series remains a significant challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs). Current research is hindered by fragmented task definitions and benchmarks with inherent ambiguities, precluding rigorous evaluation and the development of unified Time Series Reasoning Models(TSRMs). To bridge this gap, we formalize Time Series Reasoning (TSR) via a four-level taxonomy of increasing cognitive complexity. We introduce HiTSR, a hierarchical time series reasoning dataset comprising 83k samples with diverse task combinations and verified Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories. Leveraging HiTSR, we propose LLaTiSA, a strong TSRM that integrates visualized patterns with precision-calibrated numerical tables to enhance the temporal perception of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Through a multi-stage curriculum fine-tuning strategy, LLaTiSA achieves superior performance and exhibits robust out-of-distribution generalization across diverse TSR tasks and real-world scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/RainingNovember/LLaTiSA.

</details>


### [116] [Efficient Test-Time Scaling via Temporal Reasoning Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17304)
*Jiakun Li,Xingwei He,Kefan Li,Hongzheng Chai,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE是一个无需训练的高效测试时扩展框架，通过聚合多步证据而非单步置信度信号来检测推理收敛，从而减少25-30%的推理token使用，同时保持与完整推理相近的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展虽然能提升大语言模型的推理性能，但通常导致token效率低下的"过度思考"问题。现有的动态提前退出方法依赖单步置信度信号，在多步推理场景中往往不可靠，无法准确检测推理收敛。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，通过时间聚合多步证据而非瞬时信号来确定何时终止推理。框架聚合两个互补信号：答案一致性（捕捉预测答案的持续性）和置信度轨迹（建模模型置信度的时间演化）。利用这两个因素准确判断推理过程是否收敛，及时停止推理以避免冗余步骤。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准测试中，TRACE平均减少25-30%的推理token使用，同时保持准确率在完整推理的1-2%范围内，始终优于现有的动态推理方法。

Conclusion: TRACE通过时间聚合多步证据有效解决了测试时扩展中的过度思考问题，实现了推理效率与准确性的良好平衡，为高效推理提供了无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves the reasoning performance of large language models but often results in token-inefficient overthinking, where models continue reasoning beyond what is necessary for a correct answer. Existing dynamic early-exit methods typically rely on single-step confidence signals, which are often unreliable for detecting reasoning convergence in multi-step settings. To mitigate this limitation, we propose TRACE, a training-free framework for efficient test-time scaling that determines when to terminate reasoning based on temporal aggregation of multi-step evidence rather than instantaneous signals. TRACE detects reasoning convergence over time by aggregating two complementary signals across recent reasoning steps: answer consistency, capturing the persistence of predicted answers, and confidence trajectory, modeling the temporal evolution of model confidence. Benefiting from these two factors, TRACE can accurately determine whether the reasoning process has converged, thereby promptly halting inference and effectively avoiding redundant reasoning steps. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks show that TRACE reduces reasoning token usage by 25-30% on average while maintaining accuracy within 1-2% of full-length reasoning, consistently outperforming existing dynamic reasoning methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Conjunctive Prompt Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16543)
*Nokimul Hasan Arif,Qian Lou,Mengxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究多智能体LLM系统中的联合提示攻击：攻击者通过用户查询中的触发键和远程智能体中的隐藏对抗模板，在路由过程中组合激活有害行为，现有防御难以检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究主要关注单智能体模型，但实际应用中多智能体交互系统普遍存在。这些系统中的提示分割和智能体间路由创造了单智能体评估无法覆盖的攻击面，需要研究多智能体环境下的新型安全威胁。

Method: 提出联合提示攻击方法：攻击者不修改模型权重或客户端智能体，仅控制触发键放置和模板插入。在星型、链式和DAG拓扑结构中，通过路由感知优化显著提高攻击成功率，同时保持低误激活率。评估现有防御措施（PromptGuard、Llama-Guard变体、系统级控制如工具限制）的有效性。

Result: 路由感知优化相比非优化基线显著提高攻击成功率，同时保持低误激活率。现有防御措施无法可靠阻止攻击，因为单个组件在孤立状态下看起来无害。攻击在多种拓扑结构中均有效。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM管道存在结构性漏洞，现有防御无法有效应对联合提示攻击。需要开发能够推理路由和跨智能体组合的新防御机制，以保护多智能体系统的安全性。

Abstract: Most LLM safety work studies single-agent models, but many real applications rely on multiple interacting agents. In these systems, prompt segmentation and inter-agent routing create attack surfaces that single-agent evaluations miss. We study \emph{conjunctive prompt attacks}, where a trigger key in the user query and a hidden adversarial template in one compromised remote agent each appear benign alone but activate harmful behavior when routing brings them together. We consider an attacker who changes neither model weights nor the client agent and instead controls only trigger placement and template insertion. Across star, chain, and DAG topologies, routing-aware optimization substantially increases attack success over non-optimized baselines while keeping false activations low. Existing defenses, including PromptGuard, Llama-Guard variants, and system-level controls such as tool restrictions, do not reliably stop the attack because no single component appears malicious in isolation. These results expose a structural vulnerability in agentic LLM pipelines and motivate defenses that reason over routing and cross-agent composition. Code is available at https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/ConjunctiveAgents.

</details>


### [118] [Agentic AI for Education: A Unified Multi-Agent Framework for Personalized Learning and Institutional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16566)
*Arya Mary K J,Deepthy K Bhaskar,Sinu T S,Binu V P*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出AUSS多智能体架构，整合学生个性化、教师自动化和机构智能，提升教育推荐、评分和辍学预测效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育系统碎片化，缺乏跨利益相关者的多层次整合，需要从被动系统转向主动自主决策框架

Method: 提出Agentic Unified Student Support System (AUSS)多智能体架构，整合大语言模型、强化学习、预测分析和基于规则的推理，实现学生级个性化、教师级自动化和机构级智能

Result: 实验结果显示推荐准确率92.4%，评分效率94.1%，辍学预测F1分数89.5%，显著提升教育系统性能

Conclusion: AUSS系统能够实现可扩展、自适应和智能的教育生态系统，为教育AI提供统一的多层次整合框架

Abstract: Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) represents a paradigm shift from reactive systems to proactive, autonomous decision making frameworks. Existing AI-based educational systems remain fragmented and lack multi-level integration across stakeholders. This paper proposes the Agentic Unified Student Support System (AUSS), a novel multi-agent architecture integrating student-level personalization, educator-level automation, and institutional-level intelligence. The framework leverages Large Language Models (LLMs), reinforcement learning, predictive analytics, and rule-based reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate improvements in recommendation accuracy (92.4%), grading efficiency (94.1%), and dropout prediction (F1-score: 89.5%). The proposed system enables scalable, adaptive, and intelligent educational ecosystems.

</details>


### [119] [CogGen: A Cognitively Inspired Recursive Framework for Deep Research Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17072)
*Kuo Tian,Pengfei Sun,Zhen Wu,Junran Ding,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CogGen是一个认知启发的递归框架，用于生成深度研究报告，通过分层递归架构和抽象视觉表示解决现有线性工作流的局限性，在开源系统中达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成深度研究报告的方法依赖刚性预定义线性工作流，导致错误累积、无法根据后续洞察进行全局重构，限制了多模态融合深度和报告质量。

Method: 提出CogGen框架：1）分层递归架构模拟认知写作，实现灵活规划和全局重构；2）抽象视觉表示（AVR）作为简洁意图驱动语言，迭代优化视觉-文本布局而无需像素级再生；3）认知负载评估框架CLEF；4）基于Our World in Data的新基准。

Result: CogGen在开源系统中达到最先进水平，生成的报告可与专业分析师输出相媲美，并超越Gemini Deep Research。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: CogGen通过认知启发的递归框架有效解决了深度研究报告生成中的信息编排和非线性叙事逻辑问题，实现了灵活的多模态内容生成和全局重构能力。

Abstract: The autonomous synthesis of deep research reports represents a critical frontier for Large Language Models (LLMs), demanding sophisticated information orchestration and non-linear narrative logic. Current approaches rely on rigid predefined linear workflows, which cause error accumulation, preclude global restructuring from subsequent insights, and ultimately limit in-depth multimodal fusion and report quality. We propose CogGen, a Cognitively inspired recursive framework for deep research report Generation. Leveraging a Hierarchical Recursive Architecture to simulate cognitive writing, CogGen enables flexible planning and global restructuring. To extend this recursivity to multimodal content, we introduce Abstract Visual Representation (AVR): a concise intent-driven language that iteratively refines visual-text layouts without pixel-level regeneration overhead. We further present CLEF, a Cognitive Load Evaluation Framework, and curate a new benchmark from Our World in Data (OWID). Extensive experiments show CogGen achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source systems, generating reports comparable to professional analysts' outputs and surpassing Gemini Deep Research. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/CogGen.

</details>


### [120] [Logic-Based Verification of Task Allocation for LLM-Enabled Multi-Agent Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17142)
*Jonghan Lim,Mostafa Tavakkoli Anbarani,Rômulo Meira-Góes,Ilya Kovalenko*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合大语言模型灵活性与形式化验证的安全控制架构，用于应对个性化制造中的安全挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业面临产品个性化带来的变异性增加，频繁重新配置可能导致意外危险行为。现有多智能体控制架构基于预定义任务模型，难以适应新需求同时保证安全。大语言模型虽能增强适应性，但可靠性仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出一种控制架构，利用大语言模型的灵活性，同时使用时态逻辑和离散事件系统验证大语言模型生成的任务分配方案，确保制造车间安全。

Result: 通过多机器人装配场景的案例研究证明，该框架能够在任务执行前将不安全任务安全地分配，确保制造系统的安全性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功结合了大语言模型的灵活性与形式化验证的安全性，为解决个性化制造中的安全挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Manufacturing industries are facing increasing product variability due to the growing demand for personalized products. Under these conditions, ensuring safety becomes challenging as frequent reconfigurations can lead to unintended hazardous behaviors. Multi-agent control architectures have been proposed to improve flexibility through decentralized decision-making and coordination. However, these architectures are based on predefined task models, which limit their ability to adapt task planning to new product requirements while preserving safety. Recently, large language models have been introduced into manufacturing systems to enhance adaptability, but reliability remains a key challenge. To address this issue, we propose a control architecture that leverages the flexibility of large language models while preserving safety on the manufacturing shop floor. Specifically, the proposed framework verifies large language model-enabled task allocations by using temporal logic and discrete event systems. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through a case study that involves a multi-robot assembly scenario, showing that unsafe tasks can be allocated safely before task execution.

</details>


### [121] [ARMove: Learning to Predict Human Mobility through Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17419)
*Chuyue Wang,Jie Feng,Yuxi Wu,Shenglin Yi,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ARMove是一个基于智能体推理的可迁移人类移动预测框架，通过标准化特征管理、用户特定定制、智能体决策和大-小模型协同，解决了现有LLM方法在可解释性、迭代学习和可迁移性方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动预测具有复杂性和跨人群/区域的变异性，现有基于大语言模型的零样本预测方法存在三个主要问题：1）黑盒推理导致可解释性有限；2）缺乏从新数据的迭代学习能力；3）可迁移性差。需要开发一个既能保持预测性能又具备可解释性、可迭代优化和强可迁移性的框架。

Method: ARMove采用四个核心技术：1）标准化特征管理：包含四个主要特征池用于基础知识，结合用户画像进行细分；2）用户特定定制：通过自动化生成机制整合LLM知识；3）智能体决策：通过调整特征权重最大化准确性，同时提供可解释的决策路径；4）大-小模型协同：将大型LLM（如72B）的策略蒸馏到小型模型（如7B），降低成本并提升性能上限。

Result: 在四个全球数据集上的广泛实验表明，ARMove在12个指标中的6个上优于最先进的基线方法，提升幅度从0.78%到10.47%，其余4个指标也取得了次优结果。可迁移性测试证实了其在跨区域、用户群体和模型规模上的鲁棒性，可解释性分析突出了其决策过程的透明度。

Conclusion: ARMove通过智能体推理框架成功解决了人类移动预测中的可解释性、迭代学习和可迁移性问题，实现了性能提升和成本降低的平衡，为实际应用提供了透明且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction is a critical task but remains challenging due to its complexity and variability across populations and regions. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have made progress in zero-shot prediction, but existing methods suffer from limited interpretability (due to black-box reasoning), lack of iterative learning from new data, and poor transferability. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{ARMove}, a fully transferable framework for predicting human mobility through agentic reasoning. To address these limitations, ARMove employs standardized feature management with iterative optimization and user-specific customization: four major feature pools for foundational knowledge, user profiles for segmentation, and an automated generation mechanism integrating LLM knowledge. Robust generalization is achieved via agentic decision-making that adjusts feature weights to maximize accuracy while providing interpretable decision paths. Finally, large-small model synergy distills strategies from large LLMs (e.g., 72B) to smaller ones (e.g., 7B), reducing costs and enhancing performance ceilings. Extensive experiments on four global datasets show ARMove outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on 6 out of 12 metrics (gains of 0.78\% to 10.47\%), with transferability tests confirming robustness across regions, users, and scales. The other 4 items also achieved suboptimal results. Transferability tests confirm its 19 robustness across regions, user groups, and model scales, while interpretability 20 analysis highlights its transparency in decision-making. Our codes are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ARMove-F847.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Self-Improving Error Diagnosis in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17658)
*Jiazheng Li,Emine Yilmaz,Bei Chen,Dieu-Thu Le*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ErrorProbe是一个自改进的语义故障归因框架，用于识别LLM多智能体系统中的责任智能体和错误起源步骤，通过三层管道实现无需标注的精确故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统虽然能解决复杂问题，但带来了显著的调试挑战，包括长交互轨迹、智能体间依赖性和延迟错误表现。现有诊断方法依赖昂贵的专家标注或"LLM-as-a-judge"范式，难以在扩展上下文中精确定位决定性错误步骤。

Method: ErrorProbe采用三层管道：1) 操作化MAS故障分类法以检测局部异常；2) 执行症状驱动的向后追踪以剪枝无关上下文；3) 使用专门的智能体团队（策略师、调查员、仲裁员）通过工具接地的执行验证错误假设。框架维护经过验证的情景记忆，仅在错误模式被可执行证据确认时更新。

Result: 在TracerTraj和Who&When基准测试中，ErrorProbe显著优于基线方法，特别是在步骤级定位方面。经过验证的记忆实现了无需重新训练的鲁棒跨域迁移。

Conclusion: ErrorProbe为LLM多智能体系统提供了一种有效的自改进语义故障归因框架，能够精确定位错误起源步骤和责任智能体，无需标注且支持跨域知识迁移。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) enable complex problem-solving but introduce significant debugging challenges, characterized by long interaction traces, inter-agent dependencies, and delayed error manifestation. Existing diagnostic approaches often rely on expensive expert annotation or ''LLM-as-a-judge'' paradigms, which struggle to pinpoint decisive error steps within extended contexts. In this paper, we introduce ErrorProbe, a self-improving framework for semantic failure attribution that identifies responsible agents and the originating error step. The framework operates via a three-stage pipeline: (1) operationalizing the MAS failure taxonomy to detect local anomalies, (2) performing symptom-driven backward tracing to prune irrelevant context, and (3) employing a specialized multi-agent team (Strategist, Investigator, Arbiter) to validate error hypotheses through tool-grounded execution. Crucially, ErrorProbe maintains a verified episodic memory that updates only when error patterns are confirmed by executable evidence, without the need for annotation. Experiments across the TracerTraj and Who&When benchmarks demonstrate that ErrorProbe significantly outperforms baselines, particularly in step-level localization, while the verified memory enables robust cross-domain transfer without retraining.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-UAV Path Following using Vector-Field Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17995)
*Gautam Kumar,Amit Shivam,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化的多无人机路径跟随框架，实现无碰撞路径跟踪和沿路径均匀间距保持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同路径跟踪需要解决两个关键问题：1）避免无人机在收敛到参考路径过程中发生碰撞；2）沿路径保持均匀间距。现有方法往往需要集中式协调或无法同时保证碰撞避免和间距控制。

Method: 采用向量场引导律驱动无人机向参考路径收敛；提出基于相对距离和方位的旋转排斥机制避免碰撞；设计基于间距误差的速度控制律实现均匀间距；建立去中心化框架，每架无人机仅需局部信息。

Result: 通过理论分析证明了碰撞避免和间距误差收敛到零的保证；数值仿真验证了方法的有效性，无人机能够安全收敛到参考路径并保持均匀间距。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了多无人机路径跟踪中的碰撞避免和均匀间距控制问题，具有去中心化、理论保证和实际可行性等优点。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized, collision-free framework for path following guidance of multiple uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs), while maintaining uniform spacing along a reference path. A vector field-based guidance law is employed to drive each UAV toward the reference path. A rotational repulsion mechanism, utilizing relative distance and bearing between UAVs, is proposed to avoid collisions during convergence to the path, and an inter-UAV spacing error-based velocity control law is presented to achieve uniform separation along the path. Analytical guarantees are established for collision avoidance and convergence of the inter-UAV spacing errors to zero, ensuring uniform separation along the path. Numerical simulations demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [124] [Diversity Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Systems: Structural Coupling and Collective Failure in Open-Ended Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18005)
*Nuo Chen,Yicheng Tong,Yuzhe Yang,Yufei He,Xueyi Zhang,Zou Qingyun,Qian Wang,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体系统在开放创意生成中存在多样性崩溃问题：模型越强、对齐度越高，多样性边际收益递减；权威主导的群体抑制语义多样性；群体规模扩展和密集通信拓扑加速早熟收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统被广泛用于开放创意生成，期望通过集体互动扩大探索多样性。然而，这种协作何时以及为何真正扩展解决方案空间尚不清楚，需要系统研究多样性在多智能体创意生成中的表现和机制

Method: 采用系统性实证研究方法，从三个自下而上的层面分析多智能体系统的多样性：模型智能层面、智能体认知层面和系统动态层面。通过实验识别计算效率悖论、权威动态影响和群体规模效应

Result: 发现三个关键现象：1) 模型层面存在计算效率悖论，更强、对齐度更高的模型产生递减的边际多样性；2) 认知层面权威主导的动态抑制语义多样性；3) 系统层面群体规模扩展产生递减回报，密集通信拓扑加速早熟收敛。这些被表征为结构耦合导致的集体失败

Conclusion: 多样性崩溃主要源于交互结构而非模型固有不足，强调在设计用于创意任务的多智能体系统时，保持独立性和分歧的重要性。需要重新思考如何平衡协作与探索多样性

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly used for open-ended idea generation, driven by the expectation that collective interaction will broaden the exploration diversity. However, when and why such collaboration truly expands the solution space remains unclear. We present a systematic empirical study of diversity in MAS-based ideation across three bottom-up levels: model intelligence, agent cognition, and system dynamics. At the model level, we identify a compute efficiency paradox, where stronger, highly aligned models yield diminishing marginal diversity despite higher per-sample quality. At the cognition level, authority-driven dynamics suppress semantic diversity compared to junior-dominated groups. At the system level, group-size scaling yields diminishing returns and dense communication topologies accelerate premature convergence. We characterize these outcomes as collective failures emerging from structural coupling, a process where interaction inadvertently contracts agent exploration and triggers diversity collapse. Our analysis shows that this collapse arises primarily from the interaction structure rather than inherent model insufficiency, highlighting the importance of preserving independence and disagreement when designing MAS for creative tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xtra-Computing/MAS_Diversity.

</details>


### [125] [ConventionPlay: Capability-Limited Training for Robust Ad-Hoc Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18123)
*Abhishek Sriraman,Eleni Vasilaki,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ConventionPlay：一种基于强化学习的方法，通过扩展认知层次结构来包含多样化的适应性跟随者，使智能体能够主动引导团队选择最有效的联合策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临时协作中，当伙伴可以遵循多种约定时，智能体不能仅仅适应，而需要主动引导团队选择最有效的联合策略。现有方法在伙伴能力多样化的场景下存在局限性。

Method: 提出ConventionPlay方法，基于强化学习扩展认知层次结构，包含多样化的适应性跟随者。通过训练对抗具有不同能力限制的伙伴，智能体学习探测伙伴的能力范围，在可能时引导团队，必要时跟随。

Result: 在经典协调任务中，ConventionPlay实现了卓越的协调效率，特别是在约定具有差异化收益的场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: ConventionPlay通过主动引导和适应性跟随的结合，在多样化伙伴能力的协作场景中实现了更有效的协调策略。

Abstract: Ad-hoc collaboration often relies on identifying and adhering to shared conventions. However, when partners can follow multiple conventions, agents must do more than simply adapt; they must actively steer the team toward the most effective joint strategy. We present ConventionPlay, a reinforcement learning-based approach that extends cognitive hierarchies to include a diverse population of adaptive followers. By training against partners with varied capability limits, our agent learns to probe its partner's repertoire, leading the team when possible and following when necessary. Our results in canonical coordination tasks show that ConventionPlay achieves superior coordination efficiency, particularly in settings where conventions have differentiated payoffs.

</details>


### [126] [QRAFTI: An Agentic Framework for Empirical Research in Quantitative Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18500)
*Terence Lim,Kumar Muthuraman,Michael Sury*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: QRAFTI是一个多智能体框架，旨在模拟量化研究团队的部分功能，支持在大型金融面板数据集上进行因子研究，整合了面板数据研究工具包和MCP服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够支持大规模金融面板数据因子研究的自动化框架，帮助研究人员复制已有因子、制定和测试新信号，同时提高研究过程的效率和可解释性。

Method: 采用多智能体框架，整合面板数据研究工具包和MCP服务器，将数据访问、因子构建和自定义编码操作暴露为可调用工具。使用链式工具调用和基于反射的规划方法，而非单纯的动态代码生成。

Result: 框架能够帮助复制已建立的因子、制定和测试新信号，并生成标准化研究报告，附带叙述性分析和计算轨迹。在多步经验任务中，链式工具调用和基于反射的规划相比单纯动态代码生成具有更好的性能和可解释性。

Conclusion: QRAFTI为量化因子研究提供了一个有效的多智能体框架，通过整合专业工具和智能规划方法，提高了研究过程的自动化程度、性能和可解释性，有助于系统化的金融因子开发和分析。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-agent framework intended to emulate parts of a quantitative research team and support equity factor research on large financial panel datasets. QRAFTI integrates a research toolkit for panel data with MCP servers that expose data access, factor construction, and custom coding operations as callable tools. It can help replicate established factors, formulate and test new signals, and generate standardized research reports accompanied by narrative analysis and computational traces. On multi-step empirical tasks, using chained tool calls and reflection-based planning may offer better performance and explainability than dynamic code generation alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Multimodal Digital Sensing of Early-Life Laying Hens: A Pilot Study Integrating Thermal, Acoustic, Optical-Flow and Environmental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16307)
*Yashan Dhaliwal,Daniel Essien,Suresh Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本研究评估了整合热成像、声学记录、光流视频分析和环境监测的多模态传感框架，用于监测蛋鸡从孵化到20周龄的生理和行为发育特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期发育对蛋鸡长期福利有重要影响，但现有监测方法受限于主观评估和单模态工具，需要更客观、全面的监测框架。

Method: 使用150只Lohmann LSL-Lite雏鸡，在五个控制房间中饲养。整合热成像（全身温度和足部温度）、声学记录（频谱特征）、光流视频分析（对饲养员进入的运动反应）和环境监测（温湿度等），每周收集聚合特征。

Result: 热成像显示外周温度随年龄增加并趋于稳定，足部温度发育效应显著（η²=0.51）。声学特征随周龄系统性变化（p<0.001）。光流分析显示早期对饲养员反应强烈，随年龄下降（5-10周vs11-20周：t=28.12，p=0.00126）。多模态轨迹显示模态内高度一致（r=0.85-0.96），湿度与声学特征相关（r=0.65-0.70），而热、声、行为域基本独立。

Conclusion: 该试点研究建立了多模态发育基线模式，支持并行传感在精准家禽养殖中进行福利相关监测的可行性。

Abstract: Early-life development strongly influences long-term welfare in laying hens, yet monitoring remains limited by subjective assessment and single-modality tools. This pilot study evaluated the feasibility of a multimodal sensing framework integrating thermal imaging, acoustic recording, optical-flow-based video analysis, and environmental monitoring to characterize physiological and behavioural development from hatch to 20 weeks. One hundred fifty Lohmann LSL-Lite chicks were housed across five controlled rooms; thermal and environmental data were collected system-wide, while detailed audio and video analyses focused on one representative room. Weekly aggregated features included head and foot surface temperatures, acoustic spectral descriptors, optical-flow movement responses to caretaker entry, and ambient conditions. Thermal imaging showed age-related increases and stabilization of peripheral temperatures, with foot temperature exhibiting a strong developmental effect (eta squared = 0.51). Acoustic features changed systematically across weeks (p < 0.001), consistent with vocal maturation. Optical-flow analysis revealed pronounced early reactivity to caretaker presence that declined with age (weeks 5 to 10 versus 11 to 20: t = 28.12, p = 0.00126). Z-score-normalized multimodal trajectories and correlation analysis (false discovery rate q < 0.05) showed strong within-modality consistency (r = 0.85 to 0.96) and selective associations between humidity and acoustic features (r = 0.65 to 0.70), while thermal, acoustic, and behavioural domains remained largely independent. This pilot establishes baseline multimodal developmental patterns and supports parallel sensing for welfare-relevant monitoring in precision poultry farming.

</details>
